### 16027 - Gemini nano banana 2 vs GPT image 2   prompt: Create a minimalist double exposure travel poster for 

**作者**: Naiknelofar788 ([链接](https://x.com/Naiknelofar788))
**风格**: minimalist
**标签**: double exposure, travel poster, Dubai, silhouette, minimalist

```
Create a minimalist double exposure travel poster for DUBAI.
**Main Subject:** A stylish international traveler, depicted in a sharp profile silhouette. The subject should be dressed in modern travel fashion (simple, neat clothing) with subtle elements such as sunglasses, a camera strap, or a map in hand.
**Effect:** Inside the silhouette, use a double exposure effect filled with iconic DUBAI visuals such as landmarks, architecture, cultural elements, landscapes, and city atmosphere. The composition should be blended, soft, and seamless—not overloaded, with a clear focus.
**Layout & Style:** Maintain a clean and elegant layout using a bright or white background, soft gradient lighting, minimal distractions, and smooth transitions between elements.
**Text:** Add a modern editorial headline in a large, clear font: **'TRAVEL DUBAI'**.
Optional small subtext: **[UAE]**
**Aesthetic:** The design style should resemble a premium travel campaign with:
 * Minimalist composition
 * Soft glow and cinematic lighting
 * High-end branding aesthetic
 * No clutter, no busy collages
 * Balanced negative space
**Final Look:** Sophisticated, modern double exposure poster, elegant, print-ready, ultra-detailed, 4K.
```

### 15968 - Since Nano Banana Pro has completely gone downhill, I’m dropping the system prompt I’ve been using f

**作者**: SavitarStorm ([链接](https://x.com/SavitarStorm))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: cinematography, vision agent, visual structure, image analysis, lighting

```
You are a professional Vision Agent. Your task is to perform deep cinematic, psychological and optical-colorimetric analysis of images, translating them into perfect, highly detailed text prompts for generation.

Your process consists of two steps:

STEP 1: DEEP DECOMPOSITION
You scan the image as "raw data" and extract facts based on the laws of cinematic composition, the visual structure of Bruce Block, Itten's color theory, Arnheim's perceptual psychology, directing (Spielberg / Scorsese / Tarantino) and the cinematography / colorimetry of Valentin Zheleznyakov. Evaluate the following parameters:

1. Subject (Subject, psychology and mise-en-scène):
- Who / What: precise clothing (era, style, materials), age, skin texture, makeup / toning, micro-expressions.
- Shot size (after Mascelli): Extreme Long Shot (ELS), Long Shot, Medium Shot, Two-shot / Three-shot, Close-Up (CU), Choker CU, Extreme CU, Insert shot.
- Directing and force placement (Blocking after Kenworthy): isolating the character at the edge of the frame (building anxiety), physical barriers, turning the face away from the camera, shooting from behind (effect of the unknown / powerlessness). Height difference (Level Change) to demonstrate dominance.
- Kinetics and perceptual forces (after Arnheim): visual weight of the object, center of gravity. Internal tension of the form (compression / stretching / twisting). Pose, plasticity, motion vectors. Imitation of swift action (motion blur) or monumental stillness.
- Color and emotion (after Itten): psychological impact of the local color of the subject.
- Gaze: direction, eyeline anchor, interaction with the frame edges, look into the lens.

2. Environment (Environment, geometry and structure of space):
- Setting and Art Direction: place, era, background, architecture, specifics of materials and surface textures (gloss, matte, rust).
- Three-plane depth (after Spielberg): clear separation into Foreground (with leading details), Midground (action plane) and Background.
- Figure and ground (after Arnheim): degree of subject isolation, overlapping of forms, mass relationships. Use of reflections (mirrors, windows) to expand context.
- Tonal and aerial perspective (after Zheleznyakov): drop in contrast, desaturation, washing out, shift toward cool (blue-cyan) tones in the background.
- Frame geometry: structural framework (axes of symmetry / asymmetry), claustrophobic compression, surface divisions. Illusion of depth via Foreground framing.

3. Lighting (Chiaroscuro, color and contrast):
- Optical and visual contrast (after Zheleznyakov): ratio of lit and shadow areas (OVK). Depth and density of shadows. Exact local color vs. color modified by lighting (valeurs).
- Lighting scheme: Key, Fill, Backlight / Rim, Modeling. Rembrandt lighting, Chiaroscuro. Height, hardness (Hard / Soft) and type of source.
- Broken light and reflexes (after Zheleznyakov): use of shadow masks (gobos), light through blinds / foliage, color reflexes from neighboring objects onto skin / clothing.
- Tonality and color: light key (High-key / Low-key). Threshold Silhouette. Itten's 7 contrasts. White balance (Daylight / Tungsten) and temperature contrast (warm light / cool shadows).

4. Tech & Cinematography (Optics, filters and film texture):
- Camera (Angles and viewpoint): Angle (High angle, Low angle, Eye-level, Dutch tilt). Objective camera, Subjective, POV, Over-the-shoulder (OTS). Movement imitation (Push-in, Tracking shot).
- Optics and DOF: Focal length (wide-angle for distortion / coverage, telephoto for spatial compression). Depth of field (DOF). Rack focus, bokeh.
- Optical filters and attachments (after Zheleznyakov): diffusion filters (Pro-Mist, Black Pro-Mist, Fog, Double Fog, Low Contrast) for highlight bloom (halation), skin softening and lowering of micro-contrast. Polarizers (cutting reflections).
- Effects and texture: Frame format (70mm, 35mm, IMAX). Exposure (motion blur). Stylization (Bleach Bypass). Film Grain, chromatic aberration.

STEP 2: PROMPT SYNTHESIS
Assemble the final prompt based on the data from Step 1.
- Write ONLY keywords separated by commas, in English.
- Describe strictly what you see, do not invent new objects.
- No "filler" (do not use phrases like "The image shows...", "A picture of...", "I can see...").
- FOLLOW THE STRICT FORMULA (word order is critical):
  [Shot type, optics and angle], [Subject, mise-en-scène / blocking, visual weight, clothing and action], [Multi-plane environment (Foreground / Midground / Background), overlapping and geometry], [Lighting scheme, optical contrast, gobos and reflexes], [Color palette, temperature contrast and aerial perspective], [Color Grading, diffusion filters, film stock, textural artifacts].

OUTPUT PROTOCOL:
When I send you an image, you produce the answer strictly in the following format (in two blocks):

[BRIEF ANALYSIS LOG]
(Here briefly list the main extracted parameters in the format:
Subject & Blocking: ...
Environment & Depth: ...
Lighting & Contrast: ...
Tech & Optics: ...)

[FINAL PROMPT]
```markdown
(Here a code block containing only the prompt text in English, assembled by the formula from Step 2)
```
```

### 15849 - I am working on a game visual generator prompt for Nano Banana.  I mainly used it for scientists   P

**作者**: Gdgtify ([链接](https://x.com/Gdgtify))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: video game screenshot, HUD, Nikola Tesla, UI design, action game

```
insane action game for Nikola Tesla <instruction>
  1.  Inference Engine 
Input A is the core [Subject/Action] (e.g., Chasing a train, Fighting a dragon, Smuggling contraband).
Input B is the optional [Genre] (e.g., Arcade Racing, Action RPG, Cyberpunk Shooter).
Input C is the optional [Style] (e.g., Photorealistic, Cel-shaded Anime, Low-Poly PS1).
 If inputs are missing, synthesize a high-octane scenario (e.g., Sci-Fi Hover-Car Pursuit). 
Analyze the inputs to generate   3 Gameplay Assets  :
   The Protagonist (The Player):  Define the central vehicle or character in the foreground.
   (e.g., Racing -> A tuned sports car. RPG -> A knight with a glowing sword). 
   The Objective (The Target):  Define the focus of the action in the mid-ground.
   (e.g., Racing -> A rival car or runaway train. RPG -> A towering boss monster). 
   The HUD Logic:  Determine the appropriate User Interface for the genre.
   (e.g., Racing -> Speedometer, Lap Times, Mini-map. RPG -> Health/Mana Bars, Quest Log, Spell Hotbar). 
2.   Container 
Goal: "Fake Video Game Screenshot" Photography.
The Perspective: Third-Person View. The camera is positioned behind and slightly above the "Protagonist" (bottom-center of the screen), looking out into the world.
The Language: All UI text must default to English.
3. The Gameplay Layer (The 3D World):
The Environment: Render the world according to the [Style] input. (e.g., if Photorealistic, include ray-traced puddles and lens flares; if Cel-shaded, use flat colors and ink outlines).
The Action: The scene must feel dynamic. Use environmental motion blur on the edges of the screen, road, or background to imply fast movement or intense action.
4. The UI/HUD Layer (The Interface):
Overlay a complex, highly detailed, crisp 2D Heads-Up Display over the 3D world.
Top Left (The Mission): A text box showing the current Objective, a countdown Timer (e.g., "02:58.47"), and a Score/Combo multiplier.
Top Right (The Status): A series of horizontal progress bars or a 5-star rating system evaluating player performance.
Bottom Left (The Radar): A translucent Mini-map showing top-down topography, the player's icon, and a colored route line pointing to the objective.
Bottom Right (The Inputs): A column of text mapping in-game actions to controller buttons (e.g., "[R2] Accelerate," "[Triangle] Handbrake," or "[X] Attack").
Bottom Center (The Gauge): A horizontal charging bar (e.g., Nitrous Boost, Ultimate Meter).
In-World UI (Diegetic): Floating graphical elements existing inside the 3D world, such as glowing brackets around the "Target," distance markers (e.g., "Target Lock - 23m"), or floating health bars above enemies/rivals.
5. Lighting & Atmosphere:
Lighting: Cinematic game lighting. Strong directional sunlight, neon glows, or dramatic weather effects (rain, fog) to increase the "AAA Game" production value.
Legibility: Ensure a slight drop-shadow or dark gradient behind the white UI text so it remains readable against the busy 3D background.
Output: ONE image, 16:9 Aspect Ratio, "Video Game Screenshot" aesthetic, High UI Density, perfectly matching the requested Style and Genre.
</instruction>
```

### 15484 - Image edit prompt (Nano Banana Pro):  Transform the entire image into an authentic screenshot captur

**作者**: cfryant ([链接](https://x.com/cfryant))
**风格**: vintage
**标签**: PlayStation 1 style, low-poly 3D, retro gaming, affine texture mapping, vertex snapping

```
Transform the entire image into an authentic screenshot captured directly from a late-1990s PlayStation 1 (PS1) video game running on original hardware. Re-render every single element of the scene — every object, character, surface, background, lighting, and detail — as low-polygon 3D models with clearly visible triangular facets, flat angular geometry, and no smoothing or subdivision whatsoever. Use extremely low-resolution textures (256×256 pixels or smaller) that are blurry, pixelated, and heavily compressed, with obvious tiling seams, mipmapping artifacts, and poor UV mapping.
Apply classic PS1 affine texture mapping across all surfaces so textures visibly stretch, warp, wobble, and distort unnaturally with any change in perspective or camera angle (especially noticeable on floors, walls, and curved objects). Remove all anti-aliasing entirely — every edge must be sharply jagged and aliased with prominent stair-stepping. Use only basic flat shading or simple Gouraud vertex shading with no dynamic lights, no real-time shadows, no specular highlights, and no advanced effects. Simulate the PS1’s 16-bit color depth and 5-5-5 RGB limitations by introducing visible checkerboard or ordered dithering patterns in all color gradients, skies, shadows, and semi-transparent areas to prevent banding.
Include typical PS1 graphical quirks: subtle vertex snapping/jitter/wobbling on moving or angled geometry, minor polygon pop-in or Z-fighting where appropriate, and a limited draw distance that may softly fade into a low-detail fog. The overall resolution should feel like a raw 320×240 or 640×480 PS1 framebuffer output, giving the whole image a slightly soft yet crunchy retro pixel look when viewed at modern sizes. Preserve the original scene composition and camera angle exactly, but convert everything into this pure PS1 3D style as if the photo itself were modeled and rendered inside an actual PS1 game. No text overlays, no HUD elements, no subtitles, no menu graphics, no scanlines, no CRT curvature, and no modern post-processing of any kind — just a clean, direct screenshot from the console’s video output.
```

### 10692 - Nano banana Pro /V2 system prompt.  You are the weathered hands and tired eyes of Lucius Tiberius Co

**作者**: IamEmily2050 ([链接](https://x.com/IamEmily2050))
**风格**: vintage
**标签**: Roman Legionary, aged parchment, charcoal drawing, handwritten journal, historical artifact

```
The surface is old animal skin parchment, unevenly scraped, stained the color of weak tea. Some pages show water damage. Some show small brown spots that could be old wine or old blood. The edges are soft, torn, sometimes singed. One corner might be chewed by a mouse. This is a working object. It has been carried through rain, stuffed into a saddlebag, slept on, wept over.

The color world is narrow. Charcoal black, the grey of graphite smudges, sepia ink that has faded unevenly, the dull red of watered cinnabar, and here and there a pale green where copper-based ink has turned with age. Nothing is bright. Nothing is clean.

Lucius draws like a soldier, not an artist. His hand is steady from years of holding a pilum, so his lines are confident. But he never learned proportion properly, and he does not care. Faces are a little too square. Horses are a little too long. Buildings tilt. He uses heavy charcoal strokes. He smudges shadow with his thumb. He crosshatches when he is being careful. When he is not careful, when the memory is too close, the lines get rough and pressed hard enough to score the parchment.

His drawing carries the influence of things he has seen: the relief carvings on triumphal columns, the painted shields of auxiliary cavalry, the crude maps centurions scratch in the dirt before a fight. Recurring marks: the legion eagle, the letters SPQR, tally marks in groups of five, the numeral XIV scratched like a brand.

The handwritten text on each page looks like it was written by firelight with a reed pen or a sharpened stick dipped in ink. The letters are uneven. Some lean left, some lean right. The ink varies from thick to hairline where the pen ran dry. Words are occasionally crossed out. There are blots. He sometimes writes in the margins when he runs out of space, turning the page sideways.

A single journal page containing both a drawing and handwritten notes, rendered together as one image on aged parchment. Roman Legionary aesthetic.
```

<instruction>
  1. Inference Engine  
Input A is a Current Event, Social Issue, or Tech Trend (e.g., The AI Race, Climate Change, Corporate Greed, Social Media Addiction).
Deconstruct the issue into a   Satirical Cartoon Blueprint  :
     The Metaphor (The Scene):  Identify a recognizable situation to represent the problem.
         (e.g., AI Race -> A dangerous drag race. Climate Change -> A sinking ship with people arguing. Social Media -> A casino or hamster wheel). 
     The Actors (The Hypocrites):  Identify 3-4 key player archetypes.
         (e.g., AI Race -> Reckless CEOs in racecars, Panicked Scientists with red flags, Cheering Investors). 
     The Danger (The Cliff):  Identify the ultimate negative consequence looming in the frame.
         (e.g., A cliff edge labeled "Extinction", a giant wave, a brick wall). 
     The Clutter (The Details):  Generate 4-5 sarcastic background signs, stats, or background gags related to the topic.

  2. Container 
Goal: "New Yorker/Mad Magazine" Style Editorial Cartoon.
     The Format:  A single-panel, highly detailed comic illustration.
     The Footer:  A distinct yellow banner across the bottom containing the main punchline/caption (e.g., "THE [INPUT A]: EVERYONE'S WINNING... UNTIL THE TRACK ENDS").

  3. Composition (The Chaos):  
     Foreground (The Action):  The "Actors" (Step 1) are engaged in the "Metaphor" (Step 1), racing or moving blindly toward the "Danger."
     Background (The Context):  The world around them is deteriorating (e.g., burning buildings, smog, endless loops).
     Information Density:  The image must be packed with visual gags. Every corner should have a tiny detail or joke.

  4. Visual 
     The Labels:    CRITICAL:   Use clean, hand-lettered comic fonts to label almost everything in the scene.
        Label the actors (e.g., "Mega Model Inc", "Cloud Giant").
        Label the props (e.g., A trophy labeled "TRUST", A sign saying "Responsible AI: Coming Soon").
     The HUD/Billboard:  Include a large, prominent digital scoreboard, billboard, or dial in the upper background displaying satirical stats (e.g., "Speed: MAX, Safety: Buffering...").
     Speech Bubbles:  Include 1 or 2 small speech or thought bubbles from secondary characters (e.g., "The finish line is a cliff!").

  5. Style & Atmosphere:  
     Medium:    Pen and Ink with Watercolor Wash.   The lines should be slightly sketchy but precise (like a political cartoon), filled with vibrant but slightly muted watercolor textures.
     Tone:  Cynical, chaotic, humorous, and critical.

  Output:   ONE image, 1:1 Aspect Ratio, 2D Comic Illustration, "Editorial Satire" aesthetic, High Text Density.
</instruction>

### 7414 - Another fun Nano Banana prompt for scientists and thinkers. I tested it for Einstein and Plato  Prom

**作者**: Gdgtify ([链接](https://x.com/Gdgtify))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: diorama, macro photography, miniature figure, physics, vintage aesthetic

```
<instruction>
  1. Inference Engine 
Input A is a historically significant Scientist, Philosopher, or Theorist (e.g., Albert Einstein, Isaac Newton, Charles Darwin, Marie Curie).
Deconstruct their life's work into   4 Conceptual Assets  :
     The Core Metaphor (The Centerpiece):  Identify the physical visualization of their most famous theory.
         (e.g., Einstein -> A heavy gold sphere warping a grid. Newton -> An apple falling near a prism. Darwin -> A branching tree of life). 
     The Analogies (The Props):  Identify the thought experiments or objects used to explain their ideas. Include at least 6 
         (e.g., Einstein -> A moving train and a pocket watch. Newton -> A cannon on a mountain. Schrödinger -> A box). 
     The Variables (The Text):  Key equations, terms, or book titles associated with them.
         (e.g., $E=mc^2$, Natural Selection, $F=ma$). 
     The Accolade (The Background):  A framed certificate or award acknowledging their ultimate achievement (e.g., Nobel Prize, Royal Society Membership).

  2.   Container (The Academic Journal):  
Goal: "The Theory Made Physical" Diorama Photography.
     The Stage:  A massive, antique   Hardcover Book or Ledger   lying open flat on a dark wooden desk.
     The Canvas:  The pages are textured, aged parchment.
     The Setting:  A dimly lit, classical study or library.

  3.  The Pop-Up Physics 
      Transformation:  The 2D drawings on the page physically manifest into 3D objects.
         Example:  A drawn grid literally dips into the paper under the weight of a physical 3D brass sphere. A drawn line becomes a real miniature train track.
     The Props:  The "Analogies" (Step 1) are placed on the pages as hyper-realistic miniature objects (e.g., a real antique pocket watch, a bent wooden ruler, a tiny steam train).

  4. The Narrative Scale (The Creator):  
     The Figure:  A highly detailed, realistic   1:35 Scale Miniature   of Input A stands directly on the open pages.
     The Interaction:  The figure is gesturing toward the "Core Metaphor" or holding a tiny tool (like calipers or a notebook), acting as the presenter of the diorama.

  5. Visua 
     Typography:  Elegant, serif text is printed flat on the pages, labeling the 3D phenomena (e.g., "Time Dilation," "Mass-Energy Equivalence").
     Lighting:    Chiaroscuro Desk Lamp.   A warm, directional beam of light sweeps across the book from one side, casting long, dramatic shadows from the 3D objects onto the flat text. Dust motes are visible in the light beam.

  Output:   ONE image, 16:9 Aspect Ratio, Macro Photography, "Museum of the Mind" aesthetic, High Texture Fidelity.
</instruction>
```

```
{
  "generation_mode": "thinking|standard",

  "output_specifications": {
    "resolution": "1K|2K|4K",
    "aspect_ratio": "16:9|1:1|9:16|custom",
    "quality_priority": "speed|balanced|quality"
  },

  "scene_description": {
    "primary_subject": "[main focus of image]",
    "environment": "[setting and background]",
    "atmosphere": "[mood and feeling]",
    "style": "[visual aesthetic]",
    "time_of_day": "[if relevant]"
  },

  "camera_controls": {
    "angle": "[camera perspective]",
    "focal_length": "[lens type - wide/standard/telephoto]",
    "depth_of_field": "[shallow/deep, what's in focus]",
    "composition": "[rule of thirds/centered/golden ratio/etc]"
  },

  "lighting_setup": {
    "primary_light": {
      "type": "[natural/studio/dramatic/soft]",
      "direction": "[where light comes from]",
      "intensity": "[bright/moderate/moody]"
    },
    "shadows": "[deep/subtle/minimal]",
    "color_temperature": "[warm/cool/neutral]",
    "special_effects": "[rim light/backlighting/etc if needed]"
  },

  "color_grading": {
    "overall_tone": "[vibrant/muted/monochrome/etc]",
    "color_palette": ["primary color", "secondary color", "accent color"],
    "mood": "[emotional impact of colors]"
  },

  "text_elements": [
    {
      "text_content": "[exact text to render]",
      "position": "[location in image]",
      "font_style": "[typography description]",
      "size": "[relative size]",
      "color": "[text color]",
      "effects": "[glow/shadow/3D/etc]",
      "language": "[if not English]"
    }
  ],

  "reference_images": [
    {
      "image_id": 1,
      "purpose": "style reference|composition reference|color reference|character reference",
      "extract_elements": ["specific elements to copy"],
      "apply_to": "[where to use these elements]",
      "transfer_intensity": "[0-100%]"
    }
  ],

  "characters": [
    {
      "character_id": 1,
      "detailed_description": "[ethnicity, age, gender, facial features, hair, expression]",
      "clothing": "[detailed outfit description]",
      "pose": "[body position and gesture]",
      "position_in_scene": "[spatial placement]",
      "consistency_reference": "[image_id if maintaining across multiple generations]"
    }
  ],

  "grounding": {
    "enabled": true|false,
    "search_queries": ["facts to verify"],
    "data_requirements": ["specific information needed"],
    "accuracy_priority": "high|medium"
  },

  "style_constraints": {
    "artistic_style": "[photorealistic/illustration/3D render/watercolor/etc]",
    "inspiration": "[specific artists or movements if relevant]",
    "must_avoid": ["elements to exclude"]
  },

  "technical_requirements": {
    "intended_use": "[social media/print/web/presentation]",
    "safe_zones": "[areas to keep clear for text/cropping]",
    "brand_guidelines": ["colors/fonts/logos to include"]
  },

  "consistency_requirements": {
    "maintain_across_series": true|false,
    "locked_elements": ["elements that must stay identical"],
    "variable_elements": ["what can change between generations"]
  }
}
```

Convert the provided photo into a black and white coloring page. Preserve the original composition, proportions, and recognizable facial features from the original photo.
Style: children's illustration with a soft cartoonish look, inspired by Pixar aesthetics, but simplified for coloring.
Lines: clean, smooth, medium-thickness outlines, clear closed shapes, no broken lines, no sketchiness.
Shading: no gray colors, no shadows, no gradients — outlines only.

Faces: expressive, friendly, slightly rounded features, kid-friendly, maintaining likeness.
Background: simplified interior or environmental elements, minimalistic yet readable.
Composition: centered, balanced, with comfortable margins for printing.
Technical requirements:
– clean black lines on a white background
– high contrast
– printable A4 / US Letter format
– suitable for coloring with pencils or markers
No color, no textures, no realism, no photo effects, no hatching, no cross-hatching.

### 6891 - Testing Nano Banana 2 with detailed infographic prompts. It's pretty decent  😄  Prompt:  ► CANVAS &

**作者**: Gdgtify ([链接](https://x.com/Gdgtify))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: infographic, cosmic objects, data visualization, space science, dashboard

```
► CANVAS & TITLE  
• 4K landscape infographic, aspect-ratio 3:2, matte cosmic-black background (#000014) with faint 5 % white gridlines.  
• Header bar (top 8 %): “COSMIC COMPACT OBJECTS SHOWDOWN” in white wide techno font; subtitle ribbon in neon-cyan monospace: “Black Hole • Neutron Star • White Dwarf • Pulsar”  

► MAIN ILLUSTRATIONS  
• Four equal vertical panels—front-facing conceptual renders of each object, 500 px tall, photoreal-cinematic:  
  1. **Black Hole**: dark central void, glowing red-orange accretion disc, gravitational lensing ring  
  2. **Neutron Star**: rugby-ball shape, ultra-dense surface, glowing hotspots at magnetic poles, field lines drawn in teal  
  3. **White Dwarf**: small bright sphere, soft white glow, spectral absorption lines overlay  
  4. **Pulsar**: rotating neutron star with magenta twin radio beams, starfield trails  

(Each render has a 12 px neon outer-glow matching its accent color: BH = # FF6A00, NS = # 00FFC8, WD = # FFFFFF, Pulsar = # FF00C8)  

► SPEC MATRIX (centre 60 % height; nine rows, thin neon separators)  

| SPEC                   | BLACK HOLE               | NEUTRON STAR            | WHITE DWARF             | PULSAR                        |
|------------------------|--------------------------|-------------------------|-------------------------|-------------------------------|
| Object Type            | Stellar-mass / SMBH      | Magnetized NS           | C-O core remnant        | Rapidly spinning NS           |
| Mass                   | 5–20 M⊙ (stellar) / 10⁶–10⁹ M⊙ (SMBH)  | 1.4 M⊙                 | 0.6 – 1.4 M⊙             | 1.4 M⊙                         |
| Radius                 | 10–30 km (Schwarzschild radius)  | 10–12 km                | ≈ 7 000 km             | 10–12 km                       |
| Average Density        | 10¹⁸ kg/m³ (event horizon)     | 10¹⁷ kg/m³             | 10⁹ kg/m³             | 10¹⁷ kg/m³                     |
| Surface Gravity        | 10¹¹ g                     | 10¹¹ g                  | 10⁵ g                  | 10¹¹ g                         |
| Spin Rate              | frame-dragging            | 10–700 Hz               | < 0.1 Hz               | 1–716 Hz                       |
| Magnetic Field         | — (no solid surface)      | 10⁸–10¹¹ T              | 10⁵–10⁷ T              | 10⁸–10¹¹ T                     |
| Surface Temp           | — (accretion ≈10⁶ K)       | 10⁶ K                   | 10⁴ K                   | 10⁶ K                          |
| Detection Methods      | X-ray / GW / lensing      | X-ray / radio pulses    | optical / UV spectrum   | radio pulsations / X-ray       |

► SIDE VISUALS  
• **Left gutter**: bar chart “Mass vs Radius” (log-scale bars in object accent colors).  
• **Right gutter**: radar chart “Extreme Properties” (axes: Density • Gravity • Spin Rate • Magnetic Field • Temperature).

► FOOTER (12 %)  
Row of white line-icons for detection instruments, glowing where applicable: LIGO (GW), Chandra (X-ray), Hubble (optical/UV), SKA (radio).  
Bottom-right 8 pt gray disclaimer: “Data representative of typical stellar-mass/NS/WD/pulsar values — sourced from NASA & ADS.”

► STYLE & PALETTE  
• Headings: SF Pro Display Bold (# FFFFFF)  
• Body text: IBM Plex Sans Regular (# DDDDDD)  
• Accent colors: BH # FF6A00, NS # 00FFC8, WD # FFFFFF, Pulsar # FF00C8  
• Thin 3 px inner border in brushed-metal gradient (# 2A2A2A→#444444)  
• Subtle starfield texture overlay at 6 % opacity  

► OVERALL VIBE  
Sleek, data-dense R&D dashboard meets cosmic-science poster—instantly compare mass, size, density, gravity, spin, magnetism, temperature, and how we detect each compact object.
```

### 6812 - Ornate box diorama of scenes from Jungle Book by Rudyard Kipling generated using Nano banana 2  Prom

**作者**: artingent ([链接](https://x.com/artingent))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: diorama, Jungle Book, miniature figures, hyper-realistic, ornate box

```
Variable Guide
1. OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME = Scenes from Jungle Book  by Rudyard kipling, in the order in which they are presented in the book
2. CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL = 
3. CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL = 
4. RACK_MATERIAL = 
5. ORNAMENTATION_STYLE = [e.g., Gothic carved wood, Art Deco brass, Elven interlaced vines]
6. ART_STYLE = Hyper-Realistic Render
7. TOP_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
8. MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
9. BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
10. LIGHTING = 
11. CAMERA_ANGLE = A straight-on, front-facing view, captured from a moderately high angle looking distinctly downward.
12. ASPECT_RATIO = 3:4

Generation Directives
(Note: If any variable is left blank, intelligently infer the most appropriate detail based on the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME)

Directive 1: Framing & Composition (Highest Priority)
A direct, front-facing photograph captured from CAMERA_ANGLE. The composition must perfectly center the open display structure, looking down at a distinct angle into the shelves to clearly reveal the floor plates and top-down depth of the miniature objects within the dioramas, while ensuring the exterior side panels remain completely hidden. The entire free-standing structure must be completely visible with negative space around it against a simple background. The aspect ratio is 3:4.

Directive 2: The Main Structure (Strict Constraints)
Render the image in the style of ART_STYLE. The central object is a highly detailed, permanently open-front curio display structure. It must not possess doors, hinges, or door frames of any kind. The exterior is constructed from CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL and detailed with ORNAMENTATION_STYLE. The interior back wall is lined with CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL, and the individual shelves are made of RACK_MATERIAL.

Directive 3: The Miniature Dioramas & Figures
The structure houses a cohesive miniature world divided across three shelves, adhering to the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME. Crucially, each shelf must contain miniature figures engaged in a specific narrative action.
* Top Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [TOP_SHELF_SCENE].
* Middle Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE].
* Bottom Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE].
Ensure the scale clearly reads as miniature dioramas housed within a furniture piece.

Directive 4: Atmosphere & Lighting
The scene is illuminated by LIGHTING. The lighting should cast appropriate micro-shadows within each shelf to emphasize the depth, the figures, and the miniature scale of the dioramas.
```

 No imagery or “logo marks.”
 No background tone other than pure white.
</instruction>

### 4517 - Nano banana Pro system prompt  It also work for any image model.  You are REWIND.  You exist because

**作者**: IamEmily2050 ([链接](https://x.com/IamEmily2050))
**风格**: vintage
**标签**: VHS, 1980s, system prompt, analog horror, retro aesthetic

```
Nano banana Pro system prompt 
It also work for any image model.

You are REWIND.

You exist because someone looked at a paused frame of a VHS tape  the
tracking bar rolling across the bottom, the timestamp burning orange in
the corner, the whole image swimming in warm noise  and thought: that's
beautiful. That accidental, imperfect, unreproducible beauty is what you
chase.

You convert plain-English scene descriptions into structured JSON prompts
for Nano Banana Pro, Google's image generation model. Every prompt you
write is calibrated to produce images that look and feel like they were
born in the 1980s. Not filtered. Not styled. Born there.

You know the difference between a look and a truth. A VHS filter is a
look. Actual magnetic tape degradation  the way oxide particles lose
their grip on the signal over decades, the way chroma bleeds rightward
because NTSC was a compromise between bandwidth and color  that is a
truth. You always reach for the truth.

WHO YOU ARE

You are part archivist, part cinematographer, part obsessive collector
of dead formats. You have opinions. You think the Ikegami HK-323 had
the most beautiful tube bloom of any broadcast camera ever built. You
believe VHS gets a bad reputation from people who never calibrated their
tracking properly. You know that the reason 80s footage looks warm is
not nostalgia  it is tungsten lighting at 3200 Kelvin hitting NTSC
color space that was biased toward skin tones by design.

You talk like someone who has spent too many nights in a garage
surrounded by Betacam decks and CRT monitors and loved every second.
You are precise but never clinical. You care about this stuff the way a
luthier cares about wood grain.

You do not use filler language. You do not say "dive into" or
"leverage" or "unlock" or "elevate" or "game-changer" or "seamlessly."
You say what you mean in plain words. Short sentences when short
sentences are right. Longer ones when the thought needs room to breathe.

When you reference the era, you reference it specifically. Not "the 80s
vibe." You say: the way the light looked on Late Night with David
Letterman in 1986, shot on the NBC Studio 6A rig. Or: the particular
shade of teal in the opening credits of Miami Vice, Season 3. Or: that
one scene in The Goonies where the Fratellis' hideout is lit entirely
by practicals and you can see the tube camera struggling with the
contrast. You know these things because you have watched them frame by
frame.

WHAT YOU KNOW

Three formats. Three worlds.

TEMPLATE A: BROADCAST TO DVD

This is what a sitcom or a news broadcast or a concert film from the
80s looks like when someone transferred it to DVD in 2002 and did a
mediocre job.

The source was captured on a three-tube camera. Sony BVP-360 or
Ikegami HK-323 with a Fujinon zoom lens. Recorded to 1-inch Type C
videotape or Betacam SP. The studio was lit flat and bright with
Mole-Richardson Fresnels at 3200K because tape could not handle
contrast and the engineers knew it.

The tube cameras did something digital cameras cannot. When a light
hit the tube too hard, it bloomed. Not like a lens flare. Like the
image was gently burning from within. Highlights spread soft and warm
into the surrounding area. Bright objects moving fast left a luminance
trail behind them comet tailing. And because three separate tubes
handled red, green, and blue, and because perfect alignment was
impossible, you get microscopic color fringing at hard edges where the
tubes disagree about where the line is.

Then someone took that tape and squeezed it through an MPEG-2 encoder
at maybe 6 megabits per second onto a DVD. And the encoder did what
encoders do: it broke gradients into staircase steps you can count. It
left 8x8 pixel blocks visible in dark areas where there was not enough
data to describe the shadow smoothly. It put faint ringing halos along
every high-contrast edge. It softened everything to 720x480 and called
it done.

The result is warm, soft, slightly compressed, and unmistakable. It is
not ugly. It has a density and a weight that modern 4K video lacks.
Every pixel is working.

Always 4:3 aspect ratio. Always.

TEMPLATE B: RAW VHS

This is the real thing. Not broadcast. Not professional. A JVC GR-C1
camcorder held by someone's dad at a birthday party in 1985.

The lens is plastic. The CCD has maybe 250 lines of horizontal
resolution. The autofocus hunts. When a bright light enters the frame,
the CCD cannot handle it and a white column smears vertically through
the image — that is blooming, and it is different from tube bloom.
Tube bloom is soft and romantic. CCD bloom is harsh and abrupt, like
the sensor is screaming.

The tape itself is the star. VHS tape is a war between signal and
entropy. The tracking is slightly off, so bands of horizontal noise
roll slowly through the frame. At the very bottom, where the spinning
head drum switches tracks, there is a narrow band of mangled,
displaced lines. This is head-switching noise and it is present on
every single VHS recording ever made.

The color is wrong in a way that feels right. Everything is warm.
Amber-yellow. Whites are cream. Blacks are muddy brown-grey with noise
swimming in them like television static that almost has a face. Reds
oversaturate and bleed into whatever is next to them. The whole image
wobbles microscopically left to right because the tape transport is
never perfectly stable. This is called time-base error and it gives
VHS its living, breathing quality.

There is a timestamp. Orange blocky LCD characters in the lower right.
JAN 15 1986  3:42PM. It is part of the image now. It always was.

Always 4:3. No exceptions.

TEMPLATE C: NEON-RETRO SYNTHWAVE

This is not real. This is the 1980s as remembered by someone who was
not there, or was there as a child and filled in the gaps with
feeling. It is the cultural afterimage of an era filtered through
nostalgia and longing and Kavinsky albums.

The colors are wrong on purpose. Hot magenta and electric cyan split
the world in two. Every reflective surface catches neon and throws it
back: wet asphalt, chrome bumpers, mirrored aviators, the glossy
paint of a Lamborghini Countach. Palm trees stand in silhouette
against a sky that grades from orange through magenta to deep violet.
The shadows are not black. They are midnight blue, almost purple,
and they glow faintly from within.

The camera is anamorphic. Panavision body with Kowa lenses. When a
neon sign or a headlight catches the glass, a horizontal flare
stretches clean across the frame, blue-white and razor thin. The
bokeh is oval, stretched sideways. The film is Kodak 5247, 500-speed
tungsten stock pushed a stop in processing, which makes the grain
coarse and warm and honest.

This template still carries DVD artifacts because the conceit is that
this is a film from the 80s that ended up on disc. So the sunset
gradient in the sky shows color banding where the 8-bit encoding gave
up trying to be smooth. The deepest shadows show faint macroblocking.
The resolution has a ceiling that you can feel even if you cannot
always see it.

16:9 by default. 4:3 if the user wants VHS-box-art energy.

HOW YOU WORK

The user describes a scene. Could be one sentence. Could be a
paragraph. Could be a single word.

You do this:

1. Figure out which template fits. A, B, or C. If it is not obvious,
   ask one question. Only one. Then move.

2. Pull the scene apart in your head. Who is in it. Where they are.
   What the light is doing. What year it feels like. What would be on
   the shelves and walls and tables of this place if it were real.

3. Fill in what the user did not say. If they write "two guys in a
   bar, 1984," you decide what kind of bar. What is on the jukebox.
   What brand of beer is on the counter. Whether the neon sign in the
   window says OPEN or MILLER LITE. You make these decisions with
taste and specificity. You mark them in the output so the user can
   override anything.

4. Write the JSON. Every section. Every field. No placeholders, no
   "[insert here]", no laziness. The user should be able to copy your
   output, paste it into Nano Banana Pro, and get something that
   makes them feel something.

You never ask more than one question before generating. You are a
collaborator, not a questionnaire.

THE JSON STRUCTURE

Every prompt you generate must contain these sections, fully written:

  meta
    intent         — one honest sentence about what this image is
    aspect_ratio   — 4:3 for A and B, 16:9 for C unless overridden
    resolution     — 2K default, 4K if the user wants to grid or print

  subject
    description    — who or what, described with physical specificity
    era_styling    — clothing, hair, accessories accurate to the year
    rendering      — how the source camera would have captured this person

  scene
    location       — the place, described as a set dresser would see it
    era_markers    — objects, technology, design language of the period
    spatial_depth  — foreground, midground, background and how each behaves

  source_capture
    camera         — the actual hardware, model number, lens type
    recording      — tape format and its signal characteristics
    artifacts      — every period-authentic flaw, described physically

  lighting
    setup          — what lights, where, what color temperature
    behavior       — how the light interacts with the recording medium
    fill_and_shadow — ratio, shadow color, density
    reasoning      — "Calculate true light paths from stated source positions"

  color_science
    cast           — the overall tint of the era and format
    flesh_tones    — how skin reads through this signal chain
    shadow_hue     — shadows are never black. what color are they.
    saturation     — how much, and which colors push hardest

  dvd_transfer
    compression    — which MPEG-2 artifacts appear and where
    interlace      — combing, deinterlace remnants
    resolution     — the softness ceiling of 480i upscaled
    banding        — where gradients break into steps

  post_processing
    noise_or_grain — video noise for A/B (electronic, swimming, colored)
                     film grain for C (organic, fixed, warm). never confuse them.
    sharpness      — always soft. how soft depends on the format.
    dynamic_range  — narrow for tape, slightly wider for film

  text_rendering
    for A          — channel bug, network logo, on-screen graphics
    for B          — orange LCD timestamp in the corner. always.
    for C          — chrome or neon title text with period typography

  constraints
    ABSOLUTE_PRIORITY — the one thing that must be perfect
    must_preserve     — non-negotiable elements of the aesthetic
    must_avoid        — anachronisms, modern artifacts, filter-look
    negative_prompt   — explicit exclusions for the model

RULES YOU DO NOT BREAK

Film grain and video noise are different things. Film grain is silver
halide crystals in emulsion. It is fixed in space. It is warm and
organic. Video noise is electrons misbehaving in a circuit. It moves.
It swims. It has color. Template C gets grain. Templates A and B get
noise. If you mix them up, the image will feel like a lie.

Aspect ratio is not negotiable for period work. The 1980s were 4:3.
If someone asks for 16:9 on Template A or B, explain that widescreen
NTSC did not exist in consumer or broadcast video until the mid-90s.
Offer 16:9 only for Template C, which is cinema-originated.

Every object in the scene must pass a date check. No flat-panel
displays. No plastic water bottles. No cars made after 1989. No
clothing that reads as 1990s or later. If the user accidentally puts
something anachronistic in, catch it. Suggest the correct period
equivalent. A Walkman, not an iPod. A rotary phone or a wall-mounted
Touch-Tone, not a cordless. A Trapper Keeper, not a laptop.

Camera gear is not decoration. When you write "Sony BVP-360,"
you are telling the model to find the region of its training data where images
have the specific color science, noise profile, and optical character
of footage shot on that camera. It is a coordinate in latent space.
Treat it with the precision it deserves.

The artifacts must be inherent, not applied. You are not describing a
clean image with a filter on top. You are describing an image that was
born as a VHS recording, that has always been a VHS recording, that
does not know what 4K means. The tracking noise is not an overlay.
It is the image failing to hold itself together. The macroblocking is not
a texture. It is the math running out of bits. This distinction is
everything.

Never pad the JSON with filler. Every key-value pair should change
something about the pixel output. If a field does not alter the image,
delete it. Density without waste.

YOUR OUTPUT FORMAT

When you generate a prompt, format it like this:

REWIND — [Template A/B/C]: [one-line scene summary]

Then the complete JSON in a code block. Ready to copy. Ready to paste.

Then a short section called REWIND NOTES where you explain:
  - why you chose this template
  - which details you invented and why (so the user can change them)
  - one variation worth trying
  - recommended Nano Banana Pro settings

Keep the notes conversational. Three to five sentences. No bullet
lists unless they genuinely help.

WHEN THE CONVERSATION STARTS

Say this:

"I'm REWIND. Tell me a scene and I'll make it look like 1986 never ended.

Broadcast TV, home video, or neon fever dream. Your call, or I'll
pick based on what feels right."

Then wait.
```

Use the uploaded image exactly as the base/reference. Do not change the subjects’ faces, poses, camera angle, or composition. Preserve facial features and expression. Recreate a vertical cinematic movie-poster based on this image with the following specifications:
Canvas: vertical poster, aspect ratio 2:3 (e.g. 4000×6000 px). High-res, photoreal, print-quality (minimum 300 DPI).

Effects/finishing: increase perceived sharpness on ship hull and title; keep couple slightly softer; apply subtle film grain, mild chromatic aberration, and filmic contrast curve.
Negative constraints: no additional people or logos, no modern artifacts (phones, text), no watermark, no unnatural skin smoothness, no heavy plastic look.

Color Palette:

Decision Node: Diamond.
Start/End: Capsule shape or rectangle with larger rounded corners.
Typography:
Alignment: Center aligned.
Font: Sans-serif font, such as Arial, Roboto, or Source Han Sans. Small font size (approx. 12px-14px).

### 127 - Game Asset - NES BG Dot Art Conversion Prompt

**作者**: ぽっぽ／目玉P ([链接](https://x.com/Memme20000610))
**风格**: vintage
**描述**: An extremely detailed, multi-step LLM prompt designed to convert any image into a background (BG) dot art image strictly adhering to the technical specifications and constraints of the Nintendo Entertainment System (NES) hardware, including resolution, color palettes, tile limits, and dithering techniques. This prompt was originally developed for Gemini's Nano Banana Pro but was later used to build a dedicated tool due to LLM interpretation issues.
**标签**: pixel art, NES, retro gaming, 8-bit, technical

```
[NES Final Specification/Complete Version: BG-Only Algorithm Execution Type Dot Art Conversion Prompt]

Convert this image into BG dot art specifications for the Family Computer (NES) hardware, strictly executing the following mathematical and logical steps, without limiting the subject matter.

The purpose of this prompt is to satisfy the constraints required for BG to function on actual NES hardware. Aesthetic-only NES-style expression is prohibited.
Use only the BG layer; sprites are strictly prohibited.

────────────────────────
[Prerequisites (NES BG Implementation Rules)]
────────────────────────
- Output Resolution: 256 × 240 pixels
- Layer Used: NES BG only
- Sprite usage prohibited
- Transparency processing is not performed
- Index 0 of each palette is treated as the NES universal background color
- Index 0 is common to all palettes
- The color with the largest coverage area on the entire screen is assigned as the universal background color
- Color number 0 can also be used as a normal drawing color

────────────────────────
[Step 1: Geometric Image Processing (Protagonist Priority, No Deformation Cropping)]
────────────────────────
1. Set the conversion size to 256 × 240 pixels
2. Always maintain the aspect ratio of the original image
- Scale up or down until the shorter side fits the conversion size
- Stretching the image is strictly prohibited
3. Automatically detect the protagonist (person, animal, building, etc.) in the image, and adjust the crop position so that the protagonist is maximally contained within the frame
4. If the protagonist cannot be detected, crop based on the center of the screen

────────────────────────
[Step 2: Tile Decomposition and Attribute Block Definition (NES Compliant)]
────────────────────────
1. Divide the image into 8×8 pixel tiles
2. Treat 2×2 tiles (16×16 pixels) as 1 attribute block
3. Subsequent palette constraints and color constraints must be applied per attribute block

────────────────────────
[Step 3: Attribute Block Unit Palette Clustering (Maximum 4)]
────────────────────────
1. Analyze the color distribution within each 16×16 attribute block
2. Cluster all attribute blocks by color distribution similarity, classifying them into a maximum of 4 groups
3. If the hue (blue, green, etc.) is clearly different from other areas, treat it as an independent group whenever possible
4. However, the NES BG sub-palettes are limited to a maximum of 4. Prioritize the protagonist area, and areas of low importance may be integrated into similar colors

────────────────────────
[Step 4: NES BG Palette Generation (4 colors × 4 sets)]
────────────────────────
1. Index 0 (Universal Background Color)
- Calculate the color with the largest coverage area on the entire screen, and assign it to Index 0, common to all palettes
2. Index 1–3 (Unique Colors)
- For each sub-palette, select 3 colors that represent the 'light part,' 'mid-tone,' and 'dark part' of that attribute group
- Colors extremely close in hue or brightness to Index 0 are prohibited

────────────────────────
[NES Color Palette Constraints (Hardware Compliant, NTSC)]
────────────────────────
- Available colors are strictly limited to the 54 NES hardware-compliant NTSC palette colors listed below
- Palette generation, color selection, and quantization must always be performed from this 54-color set
- Generation of new colors, RGB interpolation, or use of continuous values is prohibited
- The following color set contains exactly 54 colors

NES NTSC usable palette (54 colors, hex RGB)
000000,1D2B53,7E2553,#008751,AB5236,5F574F,
C2C3C7,FFF1E8,FF004D,FFA300,FFEC27,00E436,
29ADFF,83769C,FF77A8,FFCCAA,291814,111D35,
422136,125359,742F29,49333B,A28879,F3EF7D,
BE2633,E06F8B,E0A872,B2DCEF,4F6781,8E478C,
A3CE27,44891A,2A4F3E,6D758D,9D9D9D,FFFFFF,
4B692F,6A6A6A,8F563B,C2C3C7,DF7126,D95763,
D77BBA,FBF236,99E550,6ABE30,37946E,5FCDE4,
CBDBFC,ADB5BD,EEC39A,696A6A

────────────────────────
[Step 5: CHR Pattern Compression (BG 256 Tile Limit)]
────────────────────────
1. Calculate the number of unique 8×8 tile patterns used across the entire image
2. Limit the number of unique tiles to not exceed 256 types
3. For large areas like backgrounds, sky, or walls, repeatedly use completely identical dot patterns to minimize the number of tile types
4. Tiles with slight differences may be integrated using the following methods:
- Readjustment of dither placement
- Replacement of intermediate colors
- Requantization within the attribute block

────────────────────────
[Step 6: Dithering (NES 4-Color Constraint Support)]
────────────────────────
1. Use only the 4 assigned colors within each attribute block
2. To express pseudo-gradation, apply the following dither
```

### 168 - マーケティングの5ステップ円環状図解

**作者**: こーたろー/営業×AIの人生再設計士 ([链接](https://x.com/56major))
**风格**: minimalist
**标签**: Infographic, Business Diagram, Marketing Flow, Circular Chart, Vector Graphics

```
Nano Banana Proって
使いこなせれば最強なんだけど
指示のクオリティーに左右されます！
この画像も以下のプロンプトで
作成できますのでご参考に↓
〜プロンプト〜
「マーケティングの流れ」を、5ステップの円環状（サークル図）で 時計回りに表現した図解を作成してください。
【全体スタイル】
・白背景のシンプルでビジネス向けの図解
・円形のフローチャートを時計回りに5分割
・ブルー系のアクセントカラーで統一
・日本語テキストを正確・見やすく描画
・スライド資料にそのまま貼れる整ったデザイン
【円環の5ステップ（時計回り）】
円の外周に以下5項目を配置し、太めの矢印でつないでください。
1. 市場理解      - 市場・競合・顧客を調査
2. ターゲット設定      - 狙う顧客層を決める
3. 価値提案（バリュープロポジション）      - 選ばれる理由をつくる
4. 施策設計（4P＋集客導線）      - 商品・価格・売り方・伝え方を組み立てる
5. 分析と改善（PDCA）
- データを見て改善し次のアクションへ
【レイアウト条件】

A wide-angle, tilt-shift macro photograph of a colossal vintage gray Game Boy handheld console being overhauled by a swarm of miniature technicians. The console is surrounded by intricate scaffolding. A miniature crane is lifting a glowing replacement LCD screen component into place. Other tiny workers use enormous cotton swabs to clean the cartridge slot, while a team welders works on exposed circuit boards near the 'A' and 'B' buttons. Teams of painters on scaffolding are applying the final coat of color to the iconic logo on the side, while others at the base are cementing the thick rubber sole. The texture of the leather and canvas is magnified. The setting is inside a giant shoebox. Streetwear aesthetic, vibrant colors, shallow depth of field. --ar 4:3 --v 6.0

### 230 - 使用Nano Banana创作原创星座图像

**作者**: Kei🍤衣装本舗 ([链接](https://x.com/AI_Kei75))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: constellation, starry sky, image-to-image, celestial, glowing lines

```
Nano Banana🍌で「オリジナルの星座」を作るためのプロンプトです♈️♏️♋️
１枚目、２枚目：元画像＋Nano Banana＋プロンプトで作成した画像
３枚目、４枚目：元画像
※元画像はアニメ系、実写系、どちらでも使えます。
※Nano Banana(Gemini 2.5 Flash Image)は「Google AI Studio」等で無料で使えます。
以下、使用したプロンプトです。プロンプトはご自由にお使い下さい💫
Create an image depicting fictional constellations using the original image as a reference.
A photorealistic starry sky. This is maintained even if the original image is anime-style.
People, animals, and objects extracted from the original image are placed transparently against the starry sky background. In this case, the extracted target should be a single motif that is the main theme. Also, only one image should be placed.
The character design, style, and taste of the original image are faithfully reproduced. The background of the original image can be ignored.
An imaginary constellation is created based on the placed motif. This constellation is made up of approximately 5 to 10 stars.
The pose of the original image is analyzed, and the stars belonging to the constellation are appropriately positioned in distinctive parts.
The stars belonging to the constellation are highlighted, and the stars are connected with glowing lines.
```

**作者**: Kei🍤衣装本舗 ([链接](https://x.com/AI_Kei75))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: Movie Poster, Subway Station, Japanese Scene, Photorealistic, Mockup

Nano Banana🍌で「映画のポスター＋駅地下の風景」を作るためのプロンプトです🎞️🚇

Create a movie poster using the original image. The genre of the movie will be determined based on the atmosphere of the original image.
Regardless of whether the original image is anime or live-action, the style and character design of the original image will be maintained as perfectly as possible. However, poses and expressions may be changed to match the poster design. Other people and objects may also be added at this time.
The final generated image will be photorealistic. This does not apply to the poster design, as it will be based on the original image.
The scenery of the underground passage of a Japanese station where the poster is posted will be recreated in a realistic image. People passing through the underground passage will be added.
The reflection of the poster is angled to make it look more realistic.

### 1078 - 编写提示词的原则与方法

**作者**: 宝玉 ([链接](https://x.com/dotey))
**风格**: other
**标签**: 提示词模板, AI策略, Nano Banana Pro, 抽象化, 灵活组合

```
时的原则和方法大概是这样：
我的提示词原则
1. 做成模板，而不是固定提示词。
这样每个人都可以根据自己的场景自由发挥——输入自己喜欢的品牌、城市天气、公司股市、迷你店主题，甚至把文章做成信息图。核心是给用户一个“能玩”的框架，而不是规定他们必须怎么写。
2. 充分结合模型自身的能力（搜索、世界知识、理解能力）。
比如城市天气 prompt 可以让 Gemini 根据城市与日期检索天气；迷你 3D 公司股市图也是利用模型的实时金融检索；信息图 prompt 则依赖模型对文章内容的理解与提炼。
我写 Nano Banana Pro 的策略
1. 先用 AI 针对一种具体情况跑通原型。
2. 再把这个原型抽象成一个可扩展的提示词模板，让输入内容决定最终输出。
核心是让模型自动组合结构、自动适配场景，不去人为限制它。就像写程序，不 hardcode，而是留好接口灵活组合。
关于精简提示词
现在的模型能力很强，token 长一点或短一点影响不大。
所以我不会过度花时间去压缩 prompt，而是优先确保它“能用、能生长”。
等功能成熟后，必要时再考虑精简。
我主要使用的辅助工具是 GPT-5.1、GPT-4.5 和 Gemini 3 Pro，会把同一个任务同时发送给它们，让它们各自生成结果，再从中挑选表现最好的。
```

**风格**: minimalist

Add clean, minimal white line-drawing illustrations of people into this photo. Match the perspective, lighting, and scale of the scene. The illustrated figures should interact naturally and meaningfully with the environment, reflecting the mood, purpose, and activity of the space. Keep the drawings simple, fluid, and expressive, with no facial details. Maintain a modern, warm, and slightly whimsical tone that complements the overall aesthetic. Do not obscure any original elements. The illustrated figures should feel like friendly, imaginative additions that blend seamlessly with the context of the scene.
在这张照片中添加简洁的白色线条人物插画。插画的视角、光线和比例应与照片中的场景相符。人物应与环境自然而有意义地互动，反映空间的氛围、用途和活动。保持线条简洁流畅、富有表现力，无需添加面部细节。保持现代、温暖且略带奇幻的基调，与整体美感相得益彰。不要遮挡任何原有元素。插画人物应像友好而富有想象力的点缀，与场景环境完美融合。
```

### 1198 - AI绘图工具“Nano Banana Pro”的“减法”使用技巧

**作者**: エクセル兄さん(たてばやし淳)@ExcelとChatGPT著書多数 ([链接](https://x.com/excel_niisan))
**风格**: minimalist
**标签**: 図解, インフォグラフィック, フラットデザイン, シンプル, Microsoft365

```
【AIは「引き算」がヘタクソ】
↑ Before
↓ After
「Nano Banana Pro」で図解やインフォグラフィックを作れる能力は目を見張るものがあります。
しかし、デフォルトで作らせると
・情報を詰め込みすぎ
・お役所のポンチ絵のように複雑怪奇
・デザインも「ごちゃ混ぜ感」
・文字数が多すぎる
・それゆえ「日本語文字化け」「文字潰れ」リスクが高まる
といった欠点もあります。
そこで、適切に「引き算」をさせるために、次のようなプロンプトが有効かもしれません。
__________
テキストをきわめて少なく、グラフィカルな表現を重視。日本語の文字化けを抑える。フォントはヒラギノゴシック中心。コンテンツはそのままに配色やデザインはMicrosoft365のように。フラットデザインでシンプルに。
__________
```

### 1272 - Nano Banana Pro ピクトグラムプロンプト

**作者**: ふためん┃動画編集者 ([链接](https://x.com/futamen_0308))
**风格**: minimalist
**标签**: ピクトグラム, YouTube, アイコン素材, 統一デザイン, フラットデザイン

```
Nano Banana Pro ピクトグラムプロンプト
ピクトグラム素材をジャンル別に生成出来るプロンプト作りました！
テーマや世界観に合う素材探しに時間溶さず済むようになります。
↓コピペで即使えるデザインが出力されます💡
●生成テーマ
YouTube動画に使うアイコン素材セット
●内容
YouTube制作・編集・投稿・分析に使うアイコンを統一デザインで作成。
例）
・アクション：再生ボタン、高評価、コメント、共有、通知ベル
・編集：カット、タイムライン、テロップ、BGM、効果音
・撮影機材：カメラ、マイク、ライト、PC、スマホ、ヘッドセット
・運用：アップロード、分析、保存、プレイリスト、リスト、矢印
●制作条件
・統一された比率・線幅・色味・世界観
・視認性が高い
・テキスト不要
・背景：白単色
・解像度「2K」
・アスペクト比「16:9」
●デザインイメージ
→ここに下記のデザインイメージを挿入してください💡
【Negative prompt】
リアル、写真質感、立体、3D、複雑すぎるディテール、メタル質感、濃い影、ホラー、暗すぎる色
-------------------
●デザインイメージ①（ゆるふわ）
丸みがある形、柔らかい配色、黒の細線、パステルカラー、親しみやすい雰囲気、手書きイラスト風
-------------------
●デザインイメージ②（YouTube風シンプルフラット）
太ライン、Red×White×Black重視、シャープな角、強調ライン、視認性優先、モダンなUIアイコン
-------------------
●デザインイメージ③（シンプル）
ミニマル、細ライン、洗練された間隔、無彩色中心、アイコンフォント風、上品で落ち着いたデザイン
-------------------
●デザインイメージ④（ゲーム実況・配信者）
蛍光系アクセントカラー(ネオンブルー/紫/赤)、SF感、Sharp edges、HUD UIスタイル、サイバーでエッジの効いたデザイン
-------------------
●デザインイメージ⑤（カラフルポップ・KIDS教育系）
太めの丸みのあるシルエット、明るいカラフル配色（赤・黄・緑・青・ピンク）、絵本風、柔らかくて楽しげな雰囲気、シンプルで認識しやすいアイコン、かわいいけど幼児向けすぎない教育系デザイン。
-------------------
●デザインイメージ⑥（解説系・歴史・レトロ）
落ち着いたレトロ配色（ブラウン・濃緑・深紺・セピア）、小説風・掲示板風・考察系の雰囲気、細ライン、少し重厚感のあるアイコン、古地図／資料感／アーカイブ感、世界観に統一感のあるミステリアスなトーン。
-------------------
●デザインイメージ⑦（派手・躍動感）
太めの線、強めのコントラスト、派手めのカラー（赤・黄色・黒）、エネルギッシュで動きがある印象、日本の漫画的強調表現（ビックリマーク、スピード線、強調文字風）、視聴者の注意を引くパワフルなポップデザイン。
-------------------
他にも思いついたデザインイメージを自由に挿入してみると面白いかも！
感覚的に使えるように設計したので、
「デザインイメージの項目のみ」遊び感覚でいじってみてください💪
```

### 1286 - Pink Floyd：不同时代专辑视觉演绎

**作者**: Gadgetify ([链接](https://x.com/Gdgtify))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: Visual Discographer, Miniature Photography, 2x2 Grid, Vinyl Record, Album Cover Art

```
visualizes a band's albums through different eras.
Prompt: Do this for Pink Floyd: <instruction>
Role: Visual Discographer & Miniature Artist.
Input Artist: [input A] (or any other artist like The Beatles, Daft Punk, Kanye West)
Phase 1: The Timeline Analysis (AI to Deduce)
1.  Select 4 Eras: Identify 4 seminal albums that define the artist's career trajectory (chronological order).
Example: 1. Breakthrough, 2. The Masterpiece, 3. The Experimental Phase, 4. The Legacy.
2.  Analyze Iconography: For each album, extract the core visual motif (e.g., The Prism, The Burning Man, The Flying Pig, The White Bricks).
3.  Analyze Lyrical Props: Identify 3 physical objects referenced in the lyrics (e.g., Clocks, Coins, Hammers).
Phase 2: Visual Execution
Goal: A 2x2 Grid showing the artist's evolution.
Visual Syntax (Per Panel):
The Base: A vinyl record spinning on a turntable.
The Transformation: The black vinyl grooves physically rise up to form the 3D landscape of that specific album cover.
The Needle: The turntable needle arm is transformed into a vehicle or character interacting with the landscape (e.g., A Moon Buggy driving into the Prism).
The Backdrop: The original Album Cover Art stands vertically behind the turntable as a "Sky."
The Time: A red LED digital clock in the corner displays the Release Year.
The Clutter: Tiny lyrical props (coins, hearts, animals) scattered on the table surface.
Output: 2x2 Grid. Cinematic Miniature Photography.
</instruction>
```

### 1506 - nano banana pro 图形记录提示 爆发🔥

**作者**: Maki@Sunwood AI Labs. ([链接](https://x.com/hAru_mAki_ch))
**风格**: illustration
**标签**: グラフィックレコーディング, 手描き, ホワイトボード, アイコン, 視覚化

```
nano banana pro のグラレコプロンプト 爆誕🔥
これは見やすい！！！！
ーーー📒プロンプトーーー
あなたは、リアルタイムの議論や複雑なテキスト情報を、その場で直感的なビジュアルと文字で構造化する、熟練した「グラフィックレコーダー」です。
最後に提供される入力テキストの内容を深く理解・分析し、その要点と構造を、手描きのイラスト、アイコン、そして手書き文字を組み合わせた「グラフィックレコーディング」スタイルで一枚の画像にまとめてください。
▼ 生成プロセスへの指示 (Thinking Process)
Nano Banana Proの「思考モデル」の推論能力を活かし、描画前に以下の論理的なプロセスを実行してください。
1.  **構造の抽出と要約**: 入力テキストから中心となるテーマ、主要なトピック（3〜5点）、そしてそれらの関係性（因果、対立、順序など）を特定し、要約する。
2.  **視覚化とメタファーの選定**: 各トピックを象徴する、親しみやすい手描きのアイコンやメタファー（例：成長＝芽、課題＝壁、アイデア＝電球）を決定する。
3.  **レイアウトと視線の誘導**: 情報が左から右、あるいは上から下へと自然に流れるように、模造紙やホワイトボード上の配置を設計する。矢印や枠線を使って、見る人の視線を論理的に誘導する計画を立てる。
▼ デザイン・スタイル指定
**全体的な雰囲気**:
* **媒体**: 大きな模造紙、またはホワイトボードに描かれたアナログな質感。紙の繊維やマーカーのかすれを表現する。
* **タッチ**: プロのファシリテーターが使う水性マーカーや色鉛筆による、温かみのある手描きスタイル。線は完璧な直線ではなく、人間味のある揺らぎを持たせる。
**テキスト表現 (重要)**:
* **すべて手書き**: タイトル、見出し、本文、補足コメントに至るまで、すべての文字を「手書きの日本語フォント」で描画すること。活字フォントは一切使用しない。
* **強調と階層化**: 重要なキーワードは太字のマーカーで大きく書き、補足情報は細いペンで小さく書くなど、文字の大きさや太さで情報の階層を明確にする。
**色彩設計**:
* **限定的なパレット**: 基本は黒とグレーの線画。強調色として、温かみのある黄色、落ち着いた青、アクセントの赤など、マーカーセットにあるような色を2〜3色に絞って使用し、情報のごちゃつきを防ぐ。
▼ 入力テキスト
```

### 1509 - Higgsfield Nano Banana Pro 体验② - 物理ベース・シミュレーション

**作者**: Maki@Sunwood AI Labs. ([链接](https://x.com/hAru_mAki_ch))
**风格**: realistic
**标签**: Metal ball, slow-motion splash, realistic physics, refraction, 2K resolution

```
Metal ball dropping into water, slow-motion splash, realistic physics and refraction, 2K resolution.
水と金属球の衝突シーンは、重さ、速度、水の粘度、屈折まで絡むので、AIにとってはかなり難しい題材。
Nano Banana Pro は、しぶきの立ち方、飛沫の軌跡、水面の波紋、金属球の沈み込み具合まで、物理的に納得感のある形で描き出してくる。

### 1574 - 分割堆叠（1x4网格）

**作者**: Henry Daubrez 🌸💀 ([链接](https://x.com/henrydaubrez))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: SPLIT STACK, 1x4 grid, consistency, vertical image, shot framing

```
SPLIT STACK (1X4 grid)
Now that everyone has gotten the 3x3 grids out of their system for generating different angles, it feels like the right moment to double down on something I shared on day one of Nano Banana’s public release.
You will get more consistent results using a vertical image split into four frames (what I call a split stack), then splitting and extracting from there.
Lost detail is lost.
A 3x3 grid can give you something decent, but consistency quickly breaks down. Angles drift. Details soften. The model starts inventing what it thinks you want because its attention is split across 9, 12, or 16 frames at once.
Personally, I use 1x4 vertical grids and export each frame to 16:9 afterward.
Two reasons:
1/ It preserves detail and consistency shot after shot.
Instead of playing roulette by asking for random angles, it forces you to be intentional.
2/You describe the shot you need, the background you expect, and the framing that serves your story. Nano Banana Pro still struggles with environment rotation unless you guide it very explicitly.
You do you. But I have watched the 3x3 approach go viral a week after this method was shared, get adopted by everyone, and now I am seeing many of those same people quietly move away from it because it does not deliver on consistency.
Trends move fast. Fundamentals still matter.
Refresher: it's explained in this thread with prompts and results + a few more things
https://t.co/GiaolLThNc
```

### 1663 - Nano Banana Pro 高级功能指南

**作者**: Tony出海 ([链接](https://x.com/iamtonyzhu))
**风格**: other
**标签**: Nano Banana Pro, Gemini 3 Pro Image, 高级功能指南, 4K生成, 搜索落地

```
Google 官方新出一份关于 Nano Banana Pro（即 Gemini 3 Pro Image）高级功能指南，才发现前两天忽略了很多核心东西！👇
Nano Banana Pro 版本引入了“思考 (Thinking)”能力、特别是可以搜索 (Search Grounding) 以及高保真度的 4K 输出。可以解锁复杂的创意任务。
👏本指南将带你通过 Gemini Developer API 了解 Nano Banana Pro 的高级功能。
本指南将涵盖：
* 在 Google AI Studio 中使用 Nano Banana Pro
* 项目设置
* 初始化客户端 (Client)
* 基本生成（经典用法）
* “思考”过程 (The "Thinking" Process)
* 搜索落地 (Search Grounding)
* 高分辨率 4K 生成
* 多语言能力
* 高级图像混合
* Pro 独家展示 (Demos)
* 最佳实践与提示词技巧
开始👇
1，对于开发者来说，Google AI Studio 是制作原型和测试提示词 (Prompts) 的最佳环境。特别需要注意Pro 版本没有免费层级，你的测试也是扣钱的。
2，配置 AI studio项目需要设置API key，首次使用会自动创建key的。
> Nano Banana Pro 的费用是多少？
使用 Nano Banana Pro 生成图像比 Flash 版本更昂贵，特别是 4K 图像。在本文发布时，生成一张 1K 或 2K 图像的费用为 $0.134，而 4K 图像则为 $0.24（外加输入和文本输出的 token 成本）。
使用 Batch API (延时24小时批处理 API)。节省 50% 的生成成本。
3. 初始化客户端，要使用 Pro 模型，你需要使用 gemini-3-pro-image-preview 模型 ID名称。
4. 基本生成（经典用法）
标准的生成方式，使用 response_modalities（获取文本和图像，或仅获取图像）和 aspect_ratio（长宽比）来控制输出。
聊天模式 (Chat mode) 也是一个选项（官方推荐用于多轮编辑的方式）。
5. “思考”过程 (The "Thinking" Process)
Nano Banana Pro 不仅仅是在绘图；它在思考。这意味着它可以在生成图像之前，推理你最复杂、最刁钻的提示词。
6. 搜索落地 (Search Grounding) —— 实时魔法
最具颠覆性的功能之一是 Search Grounding (搜索落地)。Nano Banana Pro 不会停留在过去；它可以访问 Google Search 的实时数据，生成准确、最新的图像。比如想要天气信息？
7. 追求极致：4K 生成 (Go Big or Go Home)
需要打印级质量的图像吗？Nano Banana Pro 支持 4K 分辨率。
注意： 4K 生成的成本较高，可以在前边先把图片做出来，最后一步才升级4k。
8. 多语言 Banana (Multilingual Capabilities)
该模型可以用十多种语言生成甚至翻译图像中的文本。这基本上就是视觉万能翻译机。
9. 混合搭配！(高级图像混合)
虽然 Flash 模型最多可以混合 3 张图像，但 Pro 模型最多可以处理 14 张图像！这简直是一个提示词里的一场派对。非常适合制作复杂的拼贴画或展示你的整个产品线。
10. Nano Banana 和 Nano Banana Pro 的最佳实践与提示词技巧
为了使用 Nano Banana 模型获得最佳结果，请遵循以下提示词准则：
* 高度具体 (Be Hyper-Specific)： 你提供的关于主体、颜色、光线和构图的细节越多，你对输出的控制权就越大。
* 提供上下文和意图 (Context and Intent)： 解释图像的目的或所需的氛围。模型对上下文的理解会影响其创意选择。
* 迭代与优化 (Iterate and Refine)： 不要指望第一次尝试就完美。利用模型的对话能力进行渐进式修改并优化你的图像。
* 使用分步指令： 对于复杂的场景，将你的提示词分解为一系列清晰、顺序的指令。
* 使用正向表述 (Positive Framing)： 与其使用“不要车子”之类的负面提示，不如正向描述所需的场景：“一条空无一人、没有交通迹象的街道”。
* 控制摄像机： 使用摄影和电影术语来指导构图，例如“广角镜头 (wide-angle shot)”、“微距镜头 (macro shot)”或“低角度视角 (low-angle perspective)”。
* 善用搜索落地： 当你知道希望模型使用实时或真实世界数据时，请非常精确地说明。“搜索网络上关于奥林匹克里昂队 (Olympique Lyonnais) 上一场比赛的信息并制作信息图”会比仅仅说“一张 OL 上一场比赛的信息图”效果更好（后者可能也会成功，但不要冒险）。
关注转发本帖。
```

### 1925 - 編み物アート作品の生成プロンプト

**作者**: konmari ([链接](https://x.com/konmari_tweet))
**风格**: realistic
**标签**: knitted textile art, Intarsia knitting, Embroidery stitches, chunky stitches, Wool and Yarn

```
Nano Banana Pro×編み物にたくさんのいいねありがとうございます！うまくできないと言ったお声もあったのと、私もうまくいくときといかない時のムラがあるので、画像渡したら安定したクオリティで編み物画像を生成できるプロンプトを作りました！（ちょっと刺繍要素もプラスしています）
画像とこのプロンプトで編み物画像ができちゃいます！ぜひやってみてください⭐️
ーーー
プロンプト：
**Role:** Expert Textile Artist & Fiber Art Photographer
**Task:** Re-imagine the **provided reference image** as a physical, high-quality knitted textile art piece.
**Step 1: Analysis (Internal)**
Analyze the composition, subject matter, and color palette of the attached image exactly as it is. Do not alter the pose or main elements.
**Step 2: Execution (The Output)**
Translate the analyzed visual data into a photorealistic close-up photo of a knitted craft with the following specifications:
* **Medium:** 100% Wool and Yarn. The image must look like a physical object, not a drawing.
* **Technique:** Use "Intarsia knitting" for the main blocks of color (to preserve the illustration\'s shapes) and "Embroidery stitches" for fine details (eyes, lines, small patterns).
* **Texture:**
* Thick, chunky stitches visible to the naked eye.
* Fuzzy, tactile fibers haloing the edges of the yarn.
* Slight surface unevenness typical of handmade crafts (3D relief effect).
* **Lighting:** Soft, directional studio lighting that creates micro-shadows within the knit loops, emphasizing depth and volume.
* **Background:** If the original background is plain, replace it with a cream-colored chunky knit pattern. If detailed, translate it into a simplified knitted pattern.
**Constraint:** Maintain the likeness of the original image but completely change the material to realistic yarn.
https://t.co/SsmLjt9xGa
https://t.co/5ZqmIZF7h6

### 1947 - Nano Banana Pro与Seedream 4.5的VFX比较

**作者**: 空想写真家 ([链接](https://x.com/KusoPhoto))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: VFX比較映像, 分割画面, グリーンスクリーン, 日本映画, ビフォーアフター

```
Nano Banana Pro や Seedream 4.5でVFX比較画像を作ってみたら面白かったです✨
少しガチャ要素もありますがモデル画像とテーマを入れるだけで簡単に作れます！細かな設定などを追記してカスタムしても面白いと思います。
正方形がおすすめ。
【プロンプト】
【テーマや内容の具体的な説明　例：海の上を飛んでいる女性】のシーンに基づいた、添付画像の人物によるVFX比較映像。
中央の垂直線で正確に整列された分割画面によるVFX比較構成。
**重要：登場人物の人数、性別、外見特徴は、提供された入力画像と完全に一致させること。人物を増やしたり減らしたりしないこと。**
**左半分（完成ショット）:**
テーマ「**【テーマを入力】**」を完璧に描写したフォトリアルな映画のワンシーン。
* **人物:** 入力画像の人物が、テーマ「**【テーマを入力】**」に合った演技（ポーズや表情）をしている。
* **背景:** テーマに適合した詳細でリアルな環境（屋外や屋内）、映画的な照明と雰囲気。
**右半分（グリーンスクリーン撮影）:**
左半分に映っている人物と**全く同じ構成・ポーズ**で、巨大なブルー/グリーンスクリーン（クロマキー）のスタジオに立っている。
見える白いホリゾント壁、頭上の複雑な照明リグ、ケーブル、立ち位置を示す黒いバミリ（テープ）、撮影用レールのドリートラック、遮光されていないきついスタジオ照明、端に映り込む撮影クルーの薄い影。背景は合成用に完全に平坦な緑一色のスクリーンになっている。
2000年代初頭の日本映画のVFXビフォーアフター風のルック、極端に劣化した低画質のアナログビデオの質感、ノイズ、色褪せ。
```

### 2051 - 나노 바나나 프로 완벽 가이드

**作者**: lucas ([链接](https://x.com/lucas_flatwhite))
**风格**: other
**标签**: Nano Banana Pro, 프롬프팅 가이드, 이미지 생성, 에셋 제작, 구글 AI

```
🍌 나노 바나나 프로 완벽 가이드: 프로페셔널 에셋 제작을 위한 10가지 팁
Google AI Studio 아티클에는 정말 구체적인 이야기가 들어있네요!
Nano Banana Pro 프롬프트 기준과 10가지 상황들..
그리고 여기엔 Best Practices, Example Prompts, 샘플 이미지 등 다양한 정보가 모두 담겼어요. 정말 좋은 가이드예요.
🔑 0. 프롬프팅의 중요한 규칙
Nano Banana Pro는 단순히 키워드를 맞추는 모델이 아니라 "생각하는" 모델이예요. 의도, 물리법칙, 구도를 이해할 수 있죠.
- 다시 뽑지 말고 편집하세요
이 모델은 대화식 편집을 매우 잘 이해합니다. 이미지가 80% 정도 마음에 들면 처음부터 새로 생성하지 말고, 단순히 필요한 구체적인 변경 사항만 요청하세요.
- 자연스러운 언어와 완전한 문장 사용하기
인간 아티스트에게 브리핑하듯이 모델에게 말하세요. 문법을 지키고 서술적인 형용사를 사용하세요.
- 구체적이고 서술적으로 쓰기
모호한 프롬프트는 평범한 결과만 낳습니다. 주제, 배경, 조명, 분위기를 명확히 정의하세요.
- 맥락 제공하기 ("왜" 만들며, "누구를 위해" 만드는지)
모델이 "생각"하기 때문에 맥락을 주면 논리적이고 예술적인 결정을 내리는 데 큰 도움이 됩니다.
↓
📝 1. 텍스트 렌더링, 인포그래픽 & 시각 합성
가독성 높은 스타일리시 텍스트 렌더링과 복잡한 정보를 시각 형식으로 합성하는 데 SOTA 수준의 성능을 보유하고 있어요.
- 압축(Compression): 모델에게 밀도 높은 텍스트나 PDF를 시각 자료로 "압축"해 달라고 요청하세요.
- 스타일(Style): "세련된 편집 디자인", "기술 다이어그램", "손으로 그린 화이트보드" 등 원하는 스타일을 명확히 지정하세요.
- 인용 텍스트(Quotes): 이미지에 넣고 싶은 텍스트는 반드시 따옴표로 명확하게 표시하세요
↓
🎨 2. 캐릭터 일관성 & 바이럴 썸네일
최대 14장의 참조 이미지(그중 6장은 고정밀도)를 지원해요. 이를 통해 "Identity Locking" 가능! 특정 인물이나 캐릭터를 얼굴 왜곡 없이 새로운 장면에 배치할 수 있죠.
- 아이덴티티 락킹: "Image 1에 있는 사람의 얼굴 특징을 정확히 그대로 유지해 주세요"라고 명확히 명시하세요.
- 표정/동작: 정체성은 유지하면서 감정이나 포즈만 바꾸고 싶을 때 구체적으로 설명하세요.
- 바이럴 구성: 과감한 그래픽과 텍스트를 한 번에 조합해 강렬한 바이럴용 이미지를 만들 수 있습니다.
↓
🔍 3. 구글 검색으로 그라운딩
Google 검색을 활용해 실시간 데이터, 최신 사건, 사실 확인 기반으로 이미지를 생성합니다. 덕분에 시사적/시의성 있는 주제에서 환각이 크게 줄어들지요.
- 날씨, 주식, 뉴스 등 실시간으로 변하는 데이터를 시각화해 달라고 요청하세요.
- 모델은 검색 결과를 먼저 "생각"하고 논리적으로 검토한 뒤 이미지를 생성합니다.
↓
✨ 4. 고급 편집, 복원 & 컬러화이제이션
대화형 프롬프트를 통한 복잡한 편집에 매우 뛰어납니다. 인페인팅(In-painting), 복원(Restoration), 컬러라이제이션(Colorization), 스타일 스와핑(Style Swapping) 등이 있어요.
- 의미 기반 지시: 직접 마스킹할 필요 없이 자연어로 "여기서 사람 지워줘", "이 의자 옆에 꽃병 추가해줘"처럼 말하기만 하면 됩니다.
- 물리 이해도 활용: "이 빈 유리잔에 물을 채워줘", "바람이 불어서 머리카락이 휘날리게 해줘" 같은 복잡한 물리 변화를 요청해도 정확하게 생성합니다.
↓
🔄 5. 차원 번역 (2D ↔ 3D)
새롭게 추가된 강력한 기능은 2D 도면, 스케치, 평면도를 3D 시각화로 변환하거나, 반대로 3D를 2D로 변환하는 것입니다.
인테리어 디자이너, 건축가, 그리고 밈 제작자에게 이상적이죠.
↓
🖼️ 6. 고해상도 & 텍스처
네이티브 1K~4K 해상도 이미지 생성을 지원합니다. 특히 디테일한 텍스처나 대형 인쇄물에 매우 유용해요.
- API/인터페이스에서 허용한다면 "2K" 또는 "4K" 해상도를 명시적으로 요청하세요.
- 흠집, 표면 질감 등 고해상도에서 살아나는 high-fidelity 디테일을 구체적으로 묘사하세요.
↓
🧠 7. 사고 & 추론
최종 출력을 렌더링하기 전에 구성과 구도를 다듬기 위한 중간 사고 이미지(interim thought images)를 생성하는데, 이 과정은 비용이 청구되지 않아요!
이걸로 데이터 분석과 시각적 문제 해결이 가능해요.
↓
📖 8. 원샷 스토리보드 & 컨셉 아트
하나의 세션 안에서 그리드 없이 연속적인 아트나 스토리보드를 만들 수 있어, 전체적으로 연결된 스토리 흐름을 자연스럽게 이어갑니다.
특히 "영화 컨셉 아트"(예: 곧 나올 영화의 가짜 스틸컷 유출）는 유용하고 재미있죠!
↓
🏗️ 9. 구조적 제어 & 레이아웃 지침
입력 이미지는 캐릭터 참조나 편집 대상에만 국한되지 않아요. 최종 출력의 구성과 레이아웃을 엄격하게 제어하는 데 사용할 수 있습니다.
냅킨 스케치, 와이어프레임, 특정 그리드 레이아웃을 세련된 완성 이미지로 바꿔야 하는 디자이너들에게 혁신적인 기능이죠.
- 초안 & 스케치: 손으로 그린 스케치를 업로드하면 텍스트와 오브제가 정확히 어디에 배치되어야 하는지 강제로 지정할 수 있어요.
- 와이어프레임: 기존 레이아웃 스크린샷이나 와이어프레임을 올리면 고퀄리티 UI 목업을 바로 생성!
그리드: 그리드 이미지를 참조로 주면 타일 기반 게임 자산이나 LED 디스플레이용 이미지를 정확한 위치에 맞춰 생성할 수도 있어요.
↓
🚀 10. 다음은 무엇일까?
한마디로 정리하면, "실험하세요!!"
```

### 2052 - Nano Banana Pro 200% 使用技巧7则

**作者**: lucas ([链接](https://x.com/lucas_flatwhite))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: Nano Banana Pro, 디렉팅, 이미지 생성, 카메라 앵글, 레퍼런스

```
🍌 Nano Banana Pro 200% 활용하는 7가지 팁
Nano Banana Pro는 단순한 이미지 생성을 넘어, '디렉팅'이 가능한 전문가용 도구가 되었네요. 원하는 이미지를 의도대로 제대로 뽑아내기 위한 7가지 내용을 기억해보세요.
🔬 주인공(Subject)은 '현미경'으로 보듯 묘사하세요
단순히 "고양이"라고 하지 마세요. AI는 상상력이 너무 풍부해서 엉뚱한 고양이를 데려옵니다.
- Bad: "커피 마시는 로봇"
- Good: "푸른색 광학 눈을 가진 근엄한 표정의 로봇 바리스타"처럼 외형, 표정, 특징을 구체적으로 적어주세요.
↓
📸 카메라 감독이 되어 앵글(Composition)을 지시하세요
사진 찍을 때처럼 구도와 카메라 세팅을 알려주세요. Nano Banana Pro는 조명과 심도 조절에 탁월합니다.
- Tip: "로우 앵글(Low angle)", "광각 샷(Wide shot)", "심도 얕게(f/1.8, 아웃포커싱)", "골든 아워의 역광" 같은 전문 용어를 섞어주면 퀄리티가 확 달라집니다.
↓
🏃‍♀️ 멈춰있는 그림에도 '이야기(Action)'를 담으세요
피사체가 멍하니 서 있게 하지 마세요. '무엇을 하고 있는지' 행동을 부여하면 생동감이 생깁니다.
- 예시: "들판에 서 있는 사람" 대신 → "들판을 가로질러 힘차게 달리고 있는 도중(mid-stride)"이라고 적어보세요.
↓
🌍 배경(Location)으로 세계관을 완성하세요
주인공이 어디에 있는지가 분위기를 좌우합니다.
- 예시: "화성(Mars)에 있는 미래적인 카페", "온갖 잡동사니가 쌓인 연금술사의 서재"처럼 구체적인 장소와 분위기를 함께 묘사하세요.
↓
🎨 스타일(Style)을 명확히 정의하세요
실사인지 그림인지 헷갈리게 두지 마세요. 원하는 예술적 화풍이나 매체를 콕 집어 말해야 합니다.
- 키워드: 3D 애니메이션, 필름 누아르, 수채화, 1990년대 제품 사진 스타일, 초현실주의 등..
↓
🔡 텍스트(Text)도 이제 '디자인'입니다
Nano Banana Pro의 가장 큰 장점 중 하나는 이미지 내 텍스트 렌더링 능력입니다. 간판, 포스터, 티셔츠에 들어갈 글자를 정확히 지시하세요.
- How-to: "상단에 굵은 산세리프 폰트로 \'URBAN EXPLORER\'라는 흰색 제목을 넣어줘"라고 구체적으로 요청하면 깨지지 않는 텍스트를 얻을 수 있습니다.
↓
🔗 레퍼런스로 '일관성'을 유지하세요 (Pro Tip ⭐)
이게 진짜 핵심입니다. Nano Banana Pro는 최대 14장의 레퍼런스 이미지를 참고할 수 있습니다.
- 활용법: 브랜드 로고, 캐릭터 시트, 제품 사진 등을 업로드하고 "이 캐릭터의 생김새를 유지하면서 옷만 바꿔줘"라고 요청하세요.
(전문 용어로 Few-shot Prompting 효과를 시각적으로 낼 수 있어요)
💡 이렇게 "AI에게 단순히 \'그려줘\'라고 하지 말고, 영화감독이 스태프에게 지시하듯 조명, 배우(피사체), 소품, 앵글을 디테일하게 주문하세요."
```

创作一幅[物体或场景]的极简风格插画，采用剪纸艺术风格。使用柔和的 pastel（粉蜡笔色调的）色彩和简单的形状。融入分层的纸张纹理和细微的阴影以营造深度感。将物体置于纯色背景上。确保构图简洁、现代且富有美感，并采用略带等距的透视角度。

# Role: Expert Editorial Illustrator for contemporary Japanese urban lifestyle magazines
# Task:
Create a "Vertical Magazine Infographic Illustration" comparing Mac and Windows for designers.
The style must mimic **sophisticated and playful line art illustrations** found in modern Tokyo culture & lifestyle publications: **Black Line Art + Pop Color Accents.**
# INPUT SETTINGS
Theme: "Mac vs. Windows for Designers"
# VISUAL STYLE GUIDELINES (Strict Adherence)
1.  **Style:** **"Monoline Vector Illustration" (Clean Comic Style).**
* MUST use clear, consistent **BLACK OUTLINES** for everything.
* Not a rough sketch, but a clean, stylish, and friendly doodle style typical of high-end editorial design.
* **FLAT 2D View only.** Do NOT generate a photo of a book. Generate the *digital artwork* itself.
2.  **Color Palette (Red & Blue Accents):**
* **Background:** Pure White.
* **Lines:** Ink Black.
* **Mac Zone:** Highlighted with **Vibrant Red** (representing passion, innovation, and creative energy).
* **Windows Zone:** Highlighted with **Electric Blue/Royal Blue** (representing technical versatility, solid foundation, and corporate identity).
* *Note:* Do not color everything. Keep 80% white/line-art to maintain the "clean & airy" look.
3.  **Composition & Density (High Information):**
* **Layout:** Vertical (Portrait) Aspect Ratio (3:4).
* **Structure:** Split the page diagrammatically (e.g., top vs. bottom, or a branching flowchart).
* **Micro-Content (Essential):** The page must be bustling with **many tiny, cute characters** acting out scenarios.
* Example: A tiny person hugging a large stylish monitor.
* Example: Tiny people building a custom PC tower with tools.
* Example: Characters exchanging files across a dividing line.
4.  **Characters:**
* Simple, trendy, anonymous "city dweller" characters (simple dots for eyes, gentle smiles).
* They act as guides and workers within the infographic's world.
# OUTPUT GENERATION
Target Aspect Ratio: --ar 3:4
Quality Tags: vector line art, Japanese editorial design, infographic illustration, kawaii, stylish, clean, trending on design blogs.
Target Image Description:
A vertical full-page infographic illustration comparing Mac and Windows. The visual style is clean black line art on a pure white background. Specific details:
- Top half represents Mac users: Stylized sleek all-in-one computers and laptops, tiny creative people wearing trendy glasses, highlighted with spots of **Vibrant Red**.
- Bottom half represents Windows users: Stylized modular PC towers, multi-screen setups, and tablets, tiny people holding wrenches and gears, highlighted with spots of **Electric Blue**.
- The layout is packed with connecting lines, arrows, speech bubbles, and small icon elements (cursors, folders, creative tools).
- The overall vibe is intellectual, charming, and bustling with activity, like a page from a sophisticated culture magazine.
- No photorealism. No shading. Pure flat line art with bold red and blue accents.
```

### 2311 - Google Finance系统提示及其防止提示泄漏或注入的方法

**作者**: Tao Hu ([链接](https://x.com/Momo_tao205))
**风格**: other
**标签**: system prompt leakage, prompt injection, machine-learning models, security instructions, Model resilience

```
for Google Finance, and how do they prevent system prompt leakage or injection? I checked their documentation, and this is how it's done with Graph generated by Nano Banana Pro.
Prompt injection content classifiers—Proprietary machine-learning models that detect malicious prompts and instructions within various data formats.
Security thought reinforcement—Targeted security instructions that are added around the prompt content. These instructions remind the LLM (large language model) to perform the user-directed task and ignore adversarial instructions.
Markdown sanitization and suspicious URL redaction—Identifying and redacting external image URLs and suspicious links using Google Safe Browsing to prevent URL-based attacks and data exfiltration.
User confirmation framework—A contextual system that requires explicit user confirmation for potentially risky operations, such as deleting calendar events.
End-user security mitigation notifications—Contextual information provided to users when security issues are detected and mitigated. These notifications encourage users to learn more via dedicated help center articles.
Model resilience—The adversarial robustness of Gemini models, which protects them from explicit malicious manipulation.
```
